        <script>
    function waitForBundles(bundles, alias, callback, listener) {
        window.executed = window.executed || {};
        if (window.executed[alias]) return;

        if (bundles.every(function(bundle) { return window.executed[bundle]; })) {
            listener && document.removeEventListener('bundle-loaded', listener);
            callback();
            window.executed[alias] = true;
            document.dispatchEvent(new Event('bundle-loaded'));
        } else if (!listener) {
            const func = function() { waitForBundles(bundles, alias, callback, func); };
            document.addEventListener('bundle-loaded', func);
        }
    }

    function atComponentInserted(params) {
        var scriptElement = document.querySelector("#" + params.scriptId);
        var setLoadedClass = function() {
            scriptElement.classList.add('loaded');
            scriptElement.dispatchEvent(new Event('loaded', { bubbles: true }));
            document.dispatchEvent(new Event('at-rendered'));
        }

        scriptElement.classList.add('executed');
        fetch(params.path)
            .then(function(response) {
                return response.text()
            })
            .then(function (text) {
                var div = document.createElement('div');

                div.innerHTML = text.trim();

                if (document.documentElement.dir === 'rtl') {
                    Array.from(div.querySelectorAll('link[href$=".css"]')).forEach(link => {
                        if (link.getAttribute('data-rtl-excluded') === 'true') return;
                        
                        if (link.href.indexOf('.rtl.') === -1) {
                            link.href = link.href.slice(0, -13) + '.rtl.' + link.href.slice(-12);
                        }
                    });
                }

                scriptElement.parentElement.insertAdjacentElement("afterend", div.querySelector('.at-container'));

                //if inserted content contains decorator's inline scripts - evaluate each script manually
                scriptElement.parentElement.nextElementSibling.querySelectorAll('script.use-script-element').forEach(scriptEl => {
                    scriptEl.classList.remove('use-script-element');
                    Function(scriptEl.innerHTML).call(scriptEl);
                });
                setLoadedClass();
            })
            .catch(function() {
                console.log('Error loading: ', params.path);
                setLoadedClass();
            });
    }
</script>

	      const dyConfig = {
	        'us-en': { id: '8784964', region: 'US', lng: 'en_US' },
	        /*'ca-en': { id: '9879828', region: 'EU', lng: 'en_CA' },

	        'nz-en': { id: '9881446', region: 'EU', lng: 'en_NZ' }*/
	      };
	      const dyPreconect = {
	        US: {
	          cdn: '//cdn.dynamicyield.com',
	          st: '//st.dynamicyield.com',
	          rcom: '//rcom.dynamicyield.com'
	        },
	        EU: {
	          cdn: '//cdn-eu.dynamicyield.com',
	          st: '//st-eu.dynamicyield.com',
	          rcom: '//rcom-eu.dynamicyield.com'
	        }
	      };
	
	      function getDySettings() {
	        const pathSegments = window.location.pathname.split('/');
	        const firstSegment = pathSegments[1] && pathSegments[1].length === 5 ? pathSegments[1].toLowerCase() : '';
	        return dyConfig[firstSegment];
	      }
	      function injectPreconnect(url) {
	        const link = document.createElement('link');
	        link.rel = 'preconnect';
	        link.href = url;
	        document.head.appendChild(link);
	      }
	      function injectScript(src) {
	        const script = document.createElement('script');
	        script.type = 'text/javascript';
	        script.src = src;
	        document.head.appendChild(script);
	      }
	
	      const settings = getDySettings();
	      if (!settings) {
	        console.log('[DY Prototype] No config found for this URL. Dynamic Yield will NOT load.');
	        return;
	      }
	      console.log('[DY Prototype] Initializing for Locale: ' + settings.region + ' | ID: ' + settings.id);
	
	      // INJECT PRECONNECTS
	      injectPreconnect(dyPreconect[settings.region].cdn);
	      injectPreconnect(dyPreconect[settings.region].st);
	      injectPreconnect(dyPreconect[settings.region].rcom);
	
	      // INIT DY GLOBALS
	      window.DY = window.DY || {};
	      DY.recommendationContext = { type: (window.location.pathname.endsWith('home.html') ? 'HOMEPAGE' : 'OTHER'), lng: settings.lng };
	      DY.userActiveConsent = { accepted: window.localStorage.getItem('userActiveConsent') === 'true' }
	
	      // INJECT SCRIPTS
	      injectScript(dyPreconect[settings.region].cdn + '/api/' + settings.id + '/api_dynamic.js');
	      injectScript(dyPreconect[settings.region].cdn + '/api/' + settings.id + '/api_static.js');
	
	      // CONSENT CHECKS
	      try {
	        const setOptIn = function (val, attempt = 0, maxAttempts = 50) {
	          if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
	          if (window.DYO !== undefined) {
	            try {
	              window.localStorage.setItem('userActiveConsent', val);
	              const isAccepted = val === true || val === 'true';
	              if (DY.userActiveConsent.accepted !== isAccepted) {
	                const { data, ...rest } = DY.recommendationContext;
	                DY.userActiveConsent.accepted = isAccepted;
	                DYO.ActiveConsent.updateConsentAcceptedStatus(isAccepted);
	                DY.recommendationContext = {
	                  ...rest,
	                  data: (data || []).concat(["after_consent"])
	                };
	              }
	            } catch (e) {
	              console.warn('[DY Consent] Error setting DY consent', e);
	            }
	          } else if (attempt < maxAttempts) {
	            setTimeout(() => setOptIn(val, attempt + 1, maxAttempts), 100);
	          } else {
	            console.warn('[DY Consent] DYO not available after max attempts');
	          }
	        };
	        //check for opt in
	        dataLayer.push({
	          event: 'pb.onOptInGroups',
	          groups: '2:1,3:1',
	          callback: function () { setOptIn(true) }
	        })
	        //check for opt out
	        dataLayer.push({
	          event: 'pb.onOptInGroups',
	          groups: '2:0,3:0',
	          callback: function () { setOptIn(false) }
	        })
	      } catch (e) {
	        console.warn('[DY Consent] Error setting DY consent', e)
	      }
	    } catch (e) {
	      console.error('[DY Prototype] Error initializing Dynamic Yield', e);
	    }
	  })();
	</script>

        window._visId_debounce = function(fn, wait) {
            let timer;
            return function (...args) {
                clearTimeout(timer);
                timer = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this, args), wait);
            };
        }

        window._visId_heroBanner_setContentMediaVars = function(carousel) {
            const DESKTOP_MIN_HEIGHT = 500;
            const CONTENT_MAX_WIDTH_DESKTOP = 680;
            const GRID_WIDTH_DESKTOP = 1608;
            const isLg = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1115px)').matches;
            const isXl = window.matchMedia('(min-width: 1681px)').matches;

            if (!isLg) {
                this.varsArray && this.varsArray.forEach(varName => this.style.removeProperty(varName));
                this.varsArray = [];
                return;
            }

            this.varsArray = [];
            const content = this.querySelector('.c-hp-hero-banner__content');
            const contentInner = this.querySelector('.c-hp-hero-banner__content--inner');
            const mediaInner = this.querySelector('.c-hp-hero-banner__media--inner');
            const mediaImage = this.querySelector('.c-hp-hero-banner__media--image');
            const contentRight = this.classList.contains('content-right');
            const twoStripesLayout = this.classList.contains('two-stripes-layout');
            const xLarge2StripesLayout = this.classList.contains('x-large-2-stripes-layout');

            const contentHeight = this.offsetHeight;
            const contentWidth = xLarge2StripesLayout ? content.offsetWidth : contentInner.offsetWidth;
            const clientWidth = !carousel ? document.documentElement.clientWidth : this.offsetWidth;
            const gridWidthDesktop = isXl ? GRID_WIDTH_DESKTOP : clientWidth;
            const gridMarginDesktop = !carousel ? isXl ? (clientWidth - gridWidthDesktop) / 2 : 36 : 36;
            const mediaWidth = clientWidth - gridMarginDesktop - contentWidth;
            const mediaInnerWidth = mediaInner.offsetWidth;

            this.style.setProperty('--media-width-desktop', mediaWidth + 'px');
            this.varsArray.push('--media-width-desktop');

            if (contentWidth < CONTENT_MAX_WIDTH_DESKTOP) {
                this.style.setProperty('--content-width-desktop', contentWidth + 'px');
                this.varsArray.push('--content-width-desktop');
            }

            if (contentHeight > DESKTOP_MIN_HEIGHT) {
                this.style.setProperty('--media-height', contentHeight + 'px');
                this.varsArray.push('--media-height');
            }

            if(contentRight && twoStripesLayout && mediaImage) {
                let action = 'remove';
                if (mediaInnerWidth > mediaWidth) {
                    action = 'add';
                }
                mediaImage.classList[action]('align-right');
            }
        }
    </script>
    <script type="text/javascript" class="use-script-element">
        ;(function(self){
            if (self != window) {
                const banner = self.closest('.heroBanner').firstElementChild;
                const carousel = banner.closest('.c-hp-generic-carousel');
                const setContentMediaVars = window._visId_debounce(function() {
                    if (banner._ready) {
                        banner.removeEventListener('loaded', setContentMediaVars);
                        window.removeEventListener('resize', setContentMediaVars);
                        return;

                    window._visId_heroBanner_setContentMediaVars.call(banner, carousel);

            }
        })(this);
    </script>
